Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any factorial module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 994. The projective resolution has length at most 197 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by factorial generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 994, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 197. QED.
